# Java translated to Rust

Translated 258 of 2685 methods (1 more is done another way in Horizon) and 519 of 701 static fields to Rust (18 of those call the game, through Horizon's helpers); 0 of 4 Fabric event handlers. It's in `runtime.rs`, under `pub mod java`; code that calls the game is in `game_side.rs`.

Each method is translated whole or not at all. A method that can't be (it uses Minecraft, a lambda, null...) is left out with its reason, and so is every method that calls it.

## Why methods were left out

| Cause | Methods | For example |
|---|---|---|
| Calls or uses other code that isn't translated | 1924 | `AddonMenuStation.AddonMenuStation(4)`: its class isn't translated (it's generic) |
| Uses Minecraft classes (needs Horizon's game API) | 303 | `AddonScenes.available(1)`: it uses Minecraft's `BlockState` |
| Other Java this translator doesn't handle yet | 129 | `AddonScenes.Factory.create(4)`: it has no body |
| Calls the game where Horizon has no helper yet | 33 | `ClientOnly.serverHasMod(0)`: it calls Minecraft's Minecraft.getConnection (no Horizon helper for it yet) |
| Uses inheritance (extends, implements, instanceof) | 11 | `ClientOnlySmoke.tick(0)`: `instanceof` |
| Uses other libraries | 11 | `JeiRecipeViewer.available(1)`: it uses `IJeiRuntime` (mezz.jei.api.runtime) |
| Uses translated text (Component.translatable) | 4 | `FreeLook.hint(0)`: a translated text with a computed key |
| Uses try/catch | 3 | `FirstPersonBodyCompat.registerFirstPersonModel(0)`: a try/catch (ReflectiveOperationException, LinkageError) |
| Uses Fabric API | 2 | `Network.register(0)`: it calls Fabric's `PayloadTypeRegistry`.serverboundPlay |
| Uses JOML maths (vectors, matrices) | 2 | `Picking.toGui(1)`: it uses `Vector4f` (org.joml) |
| Uses null | 2 | `FurnaceLayouts.index(1)`: a switch on StationKind (or null) |
| Done another way in Horizon | 1 | `ModMenuIntegration.getModConfigScreenFactory(0)`: done another way in Horizon: the mod's page in Options > Mods (its settings buttons) |
| Shares one object between names (Java references) | 1 | `ShelfBooks.forgetEntity(1)`: List.removeIf on a list kept in a changing static |
| Uses Java collections other than List (Map, Set...) | 1 | `FrameProbe.evenness(2)`: it calls `Arrays` (java.util).sort |

## Fabric event handlers

| Handler | Hook | Result |
|---|---|---|
| dev/dyslexic/cinematic/client/CinematicClient.java:47 | `tick` | not translated: it calls Scene.tick, which isn't translated |
| dev/dyslexic/cinematic/client/CinematicClient.java:47 | `hud` | not translated: it calls Overlay.render, which isn't translated |
| dev/dyslexic/cinematic/client/CinematicClient.java:47 | `join` | not translated: it calls ClientOnly.onLogin, which isn't translated |
| dev/dyslexic/cinematic/Cinematic.java:14 | `init` | not translated: it calls Network.register, which isn't translated |
